\DoxyCodeLine{02452\ \ \ \ \ \textcolor{comment}{/*\ Solves\ a\ specific\ equation\ of\ the\ form\ Ax=B,\ where\ A\ is\ a\ triangular\ matrix}}

\DoxyCodeLine{02455\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ *\ \ If\ A\ is\ [n,\ n]\ and\ B\ is\ [n,\ m],\ then\ the\ result\ will\ be\ [n,\ m]\ as\ well}}

\DoxyCodeLine{02457\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ *\ \ where\ n\ >\ 100\ sparingly,\ pre-\/chunk\ if\ necessary.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{02458\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ *}}
\DoxyCodeLine{02459\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ *\ \ If\ left\ =\ false,\ solves\ xA=B\ instead}}

\DoxyCodeLine{02461\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ *\ \ If\ uni\ =\ true,\ assumes\ diagonal\ of\ A\ to\ be\ all\ ones\ (will\ override\ actual\ values)}}
\DoxyCodeLine{02462\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ *}}
\DoxyCodeLine{02463\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ *\ \ TODO:\ currently\ only\ lower,\ right,\ non-\/unitriangular\ variant\ is\ implemented}}
\DoxyCodeLine{02464\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ */}}
